1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any wind module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 708. The projective resolution has length at most 990 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 708 + O(1/n^990). Summing over all modes and applying the wind convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
